! Title: AdBlock DNS Lite
! Description: 适用于 AdGuard、AdGuardHome 的去广告合并规则，每 8 个小时更新一次。规则源：AdGuard Base filter、AdGuard Chinese filter、AdGuard Mobile Ads filter、AdGuard DNS filter、uBlock filter、EasyList、EasyList China、EasyPrivacy、CJX's Annoyance List、xinggsf mv、halflife、NoAppDownload、DD-AD、banad、Ad Filter J、Fuck Fuckadblock、1Hosts (Lite)、AdRules DNS List、abp_small、AWAvenue Ads Rule、BlueSkyXN skyrules、ilpl、xiaobeita、URLhaus、DigitalSide Threat-Intel、pro.mini、cpbl-abp-list、tif.mini、qy-Ads-Rule、adblock、final-rules。Lite 版仅针对国内域名拦截。
